Model before rewriting:

letting a be {2,3}
letting b be {1,2}
find c: set (size 1) of int(1..3)

such that

and([(c subsetEq b),!((c subsetEq a));int(1..)])

--

c
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
c
new variables:
  find c#packed_1: int(0..7)
new constraints:
  sum([(c#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(c#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(c#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 1

c
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
c

c#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
c#packed_1
